- **paper-deep-read**: Explain one difficult paper at the student's level — its objective, its method step by step, its mathematics, its hidden assumptions and what is reproducible. Use when the student asks what a paper did, how a method works, why it is hard, or says they do not understand an article. ([LNdme/SUniv](https://github.com/LNdme/SUniv/tree/HEAD/deploy/layers/suniv/sandbox/skills/paper-deep-read) · ★1)
- **paper-deep-read**: Layered academic paper deep-reading plugin. Produces a layered product family from a MinerU-parsed paper — L2 machine-readable textonly digest (S1-S8, standalone-stoppable), L2.5 dual TLDR (machine single-modal + human with figures), L3 human-facing triple-column HTML (原文|缩译|分析, five-dimension highlights, inline-SVG mechanism, ten questions, typed critique), and opt-in code-assisted reading (paper-vs-code consistency + code essentials). Use for 解读/deep-read/讲透 requests, HTML reading companions, textonly digests to feed other LLMs, or TLDR generation. Ingestion (lit-cite) is a different skill;… ([Bryce-Peng/paper-deep-read](https://github.com/Bryce-Peng/paper-deep-read/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-deep-read) · 2 copies)

- **paper-deep-reader**: Use when a user supplies one or more academic papers and asks to read, explain, compare, critique, reproduce, or synthesize them. - **paper-deep-reading**: Deeply read and dissect a single academic paper from surface to core — extracting problem framing, method anatomy, experiment audit, critical evaluation, and reusable insights. - **paper-quick-audit**: 轻量级论文审核技能，融合 paper-review（6维度深度审稿）和 paper-audit（多格式支持）的核心能力。 支持 LaTeX (.tex)、Typst (.typ)、PDF (.pdf)、Markdown (.md) 输入格式。 六种模式：self-check（投稿前自检）、review（同行评审模拟）、gate（质量门禁）、 单维度审稿、Rebuttal 辅助、integrity（学术诚信门，已整合 academic-integrity-gate）。 6维度审查：贡献与创新性、方法论严谨性、实验设计完整性、文献与相关工作、逻辑与论证结构、投稿匹配度。 学术诚信门包含：引用存在性验证、AI 使用披露检查、证据等级标注、五项压力测试、研究问题压力测试。 输出 P0-P3 分级报告，每条含位置+修复建议。 触发词：审稿、审核、paper review、检查论文、投稿前检查、预审稿、 review my paper、audit paper、质量门禁、帮我看看这篇论文、 看看论文有什么问题、修改论文、这篇能不能投、 学术诚信、academic integrity、引用验证、AI 披露、 学术不端检查、引用真实性、integrity gate。 ([clonetimer/Clonetimer-Skills](https://github.com/clonetimer/Clonetimer-Skills/tree/HEAD/paper-quick-audit))

- **paper-quick-reader**: AI 论文速读 Skill：三档深度（裸读 / 引导 / 精读）+ 页码级 Provenance 防幻觉 + 多篇对比。 触发词：论文速读、读这篇论文、抓核心观点、论文对比、多篇对比、与我研究方向的关联、 第几页提到 X、这篇论文的数据集怎么构造的、论文精读、 paper summary、summarize this paper、compare these papers、literature skim、extract methodology。 不做：论文检索、文献管理、论文翻译、扫描件 OCR、论文写作 / 降重。 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-quick-reader) · ★455 · 4 copies)
